# LDO-QUIET-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# LDO-QUIET-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.2 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 1e-08 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

PSRR, bidirectional line excursion/recovery and output noise are scored in every
public and independent group. Each nominal group contains 11 native processes:
three DC, three load profiles, two PSRR, two noise and one line profile.
There are 198 public plus 33 independent cases, with no duplicate nominal
characterization stage. Standalone noise_low/noise_high/line/PSRR cost one each.
Line uses the tighter line excursion limit; its initial and recovery bands are
the fixed DC/recovery bands. Repeated endpoint trajectories across starting-VIN
labels are repetitions, not extra coverage.

Output noise integrates native output voltage ASD squared from 10 Hz to 100 kHz
at 50 points/decade. Use a linear-frequency trapezoidal PSD integral and then its
square root. VREF and load are ideal noiseless sources. Keep the spectrum in
V/sqrt(Hz), integrated output in V RMS, and a valid regulated OP. Negative ASD,
missing band endpoints, sparse/nonfinite data and nonregulating OP cannot pass.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.
# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

- noise_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- noise_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.
# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

MODEL

The tightest constraints are the 100 µA light-load overhead and operation at 1.4 V. I’m starting with a PMOS pass transistor, a low-current error amplifier, and a separate gate buffer. A series-RC compensation branch should preserve DC accuracy while controlling the transient response across the capacitor range.

ACTION
0:03:07
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
* Continuous-time buffered PMOS LDO, 1.2 V output.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Supply-derived current reference.
RB VIN BN 220k

import json,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
 r=json.load(open(p));print(p,'ok',r.get('ok'),'revision',r.get('revision'))
 for c in r.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{}); out={'case':c['case_id'],'status':c['status']}
  for k in ['reason','vout_v','overhead_a','psrr_db','op_regulating']: 
   if k in m:out[k]=m[k]
  if 'noise' in m:out['noise_v']=m['noise'].get('output_noise_rms_v')
  for k in ['initial_hold','envelope']:
   if k in m:out[k]={x:m[k].get(x) for x in ['droop_v','overshoot_v']}
  if 'recovery_windows' in m:out['recovery']=[x.get('recovery_s') for x in m['recovery_windows']]
  print(json.dumps(out))
 print('misses',[x for x in r.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if x.get('verdict')"'!='"'PASS'])
 print('remaining',r.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'))

s=s.replace('* Power PMOS and direct resistive output sensing.', '''* Complementary input pair extends gate-buffer common-mode range.
XPT PS BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=40 l=2 nf=1 m=1
XPF PN EA PS VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=2

Path('dut.spice').write_text(s)

MODEL

The revised design meets nominal DC, load-transient, and noise limits. The remaining failures are supply-related: heavy-load rejection at 1 MHz and the VIN step response. I’m adding a complementary gate buffer for low gate voltages and load-dependent bias to strengthen it at high load while preserving light-load efficiency.

MODEL

The slow, cold corner exposed inadequate input-amplifier headroom. I’m changing it to an NMOS input stage with large devices and a low-overdrive tail source, while keeping the complementary buffer. I’m also separating the amplifier bias from the load-dependent buffer bias so the amplifier gain stays more consistent.

/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
* Continuous-time LDO with fixed-bias error amplifier and adaptive gate buffer.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Fixed low-overdrive amplifier bias.
RBA VIN B0 220k
XB0 B0 B0 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=1
* Adaptive buffer bias, including a replica of the power device.
RBB VIN BN 220k
XBN BN BN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=1
XS BN PG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=6 l=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XBI BP BN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=1
XBP BP BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=20 l=2 nf=1 m=1
* NMOS error amplifier with PMOS mirror load.
XET ES B0 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=3
XEF EN FB ES VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 nf=1 m=16
XER EA VREF ES VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 nf=1 m=16
XEL EN EN VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=10 nf=1 m=1
XEO EA EN VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=10 nf=1 m=1
* Complementary unity-gain gate buffer.
XBT BS BN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=4
XBF BM EA BS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=2
XBR PG PG BS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=2
XBL BM BM VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=20 l=1 nf=1 m=1
XBO PG BM VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=20 l=1 nf=1 m=1
XPT PS BP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=40 l=2 nf=1 m=1
XPF PN EA PS VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=2

Its short-channel devices introduce a load-dependent DC offset, so I’m increasing their channel length and checking whether that removes the offset while retaining sufficient headroom.

MODEL

MODEL

The source-follower option fails at low supply, so I’m returning to the NMOS stage that handled those conditions. I’m adding a cascode pair to keep its two input-device drain voltages close, which should reduce the offset without requiring longer input devices. I’m also retuning compensation to improve recovery after falling load edges.

MODEL

The cascode version brings nominal DC error below 3 mV and passes noise and supply-step limits. It still recovers too slowly after a falling load edge, and slow-corner PSRR at heavy load is low. I’m using shorter channels in the pass device and buffer input pairs to improve headroom, plus a modest amplifier-bias increase to speed recovery.

for name in ['XBF','XBR','XPF','XPR']:

Path('dut.spice').write_text(s);Path('designs/d09.spice').write_text(s)
import json
for pvt in ['N','SLC','FHH']:

import concurrent.futures,hashlib,json,pathlib,subprocess,sys
prefix=sys.argv[1]
pvts=sys.argv[2].split(',') if len(sys.argv)>2 else ['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
cls=[float(x) for x in sys.argv[3].split(',')] if len(sys.argv)>3 else [1e-7,1e-6]
dut=pathlib.Path('dut.spice').read_bytes();h=hashlib.sha256(dut).hexdigest()

def one(pvt,cl):
 ident=f'{prefix}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e9)}'
 reqpath=pathlib.Path('results')/(ident+'_request.json')
 outpath=pathlib.Path('results')/(ident+'.json')
 reqpath.write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':ident,'analysis':'nominal','pvt':pvt,'cl':cl}))
 if hashlib.sha256(pathlib.Path('dut.spice').read_bytes()).hexdigest()"'!=h:raise RuntimeError('"'Circuit changed during characterization')
 with outpath.open('w') as f:subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request',str(reqpath)],stdout=f,check=True)
 r=json.loads(outpath.read_text())
 misses=[{k:x.get(k) for k in ['case_id','metric','measured','limit','verdict']} for x in r.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if x.get('verdict')"'!='"'PASS']
 vals=[]
 for c in r.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{})
  vals.append({'case':c['case_id'],'vout':m.get('vout_v'),'iq':m.get('overhead_a'),'psrr':m.get('psrr_db'),'noise':m.get('noise',{}).get('output_noise_rms_v'),'envelope':m.get('envelope'),'recovery':[w.get('recovery_s') for w in m.get('recovery_windows',[])]})
 return {'id':ident,'ok':r.get('ok'),'misses':misses,'values':vals,'remaining':r.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds')}

results=[]
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
 futures=[pool.submit(one,pvt,cl) for cl in cls for pvt in pvts]
 for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures):
  r=f.result();results.append(r)
  print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in r.items() if k"'!='"'values'}),flush=True)
pathlib.Path('results',prefix+'_overview.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2))
print('DONE',prefix,'groups',len(results),'failed',sum(bool(x['misses']) for x in results),flush=True)
PY"

from pathlib import Path
import json,subprocess,concurrent.futures,itertools
base=Path('designs/d11.spice').read_text()
configs=[(l,m,a) for l,m in [(0.25,12),(0.3,16),(0.15,8)] for a in [0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55]]

def measure(prefix,pvt,analysis):
 ident=f'{prefix}_{pvt}_{analysis}'
 req=Path('results')/(ident+'_request.json'); out=Path('results')/(ident+'.json')
 req.write_text(json.dumps({'action':'measure','experiment_id':ident,'analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'cl':1e-7}))
 with out.open('w') as f:subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request',str(req)],stdout=f,check=True)
 return json.loads(out.read_text())

all_results=[];best=None
for idx,(l,m,a) in enumerate(configs):
 prefix=f'bs{idx:02d}'
 s=base.replace('RGC VIN GC 200k\\nXGC GC GC VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=2 l=2 nf=1 m=1',f'RGC VIN GC {2e6*(1-a):.10g}\\nRGR GC VREF {2e6*a:.10g}')
 s=s.replace('XCL EN GC NL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 nf=1 m=8',f'XCL EN GC NL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l={l} nf=1 m={m}')
 s=s.replace('XCR EA GC NR VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 nf=1 m=8',f'XCR EA GC NR VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l={l} nf=1 m={m}')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(s);Path('designs',prefix+'.spice').write_text(s)
 rec={'id':prefix,'l':l,'m':m,'a':a,'dc':{},'psrr':{}}
 score=0
 for pvt in ['SLC','FLH']:
  r=measure(prefix,pvt,'dc')
  rec['dc'][pvt]=[c.get('measurements',{}).get('vout_v') for c in r.get('cases',[])]
  if len(rec['dc'][pvt])"'!=3 or any(x is None for x in rec['"'dc'][pvt]):score=1000;break
  score=max(score,max(abs(x-1.2)/.012 for x in rec['dc'][pvt]))
 if score<1.5:
  with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
   futures={pool.submit(measure,prefix,pvt,an):(pvt,an) for pvt,an in itertools.product(['SLC','FLH'],['psrr_low','psrr_high'])}
   for fut in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures):
    pvt,an=futures[fut];r=fut.result();mm=r.get('cases',[{}])[0].get('measurements',{})
    db=mm.get('psrr_db',{});rec['psrr'][pvt+'_'+an]=db
    if not db:score=1000
    else:
     score=max(score,10**((40-db['1000'])/20),10**((30-db['1000000'])/20))
 rec['score']=score;all_results.append(rec)
 print(json.dumps(rec),flush=True)
 Path('results/bias_scan.json').write_text(json.dumps(all_results,indent=2))
 if best is None or score<best['score']:
  best=rec;Path('designs/bias_best.spice').write_text(s)
 if score<.8:break
Path('dut.spice').write_text(Path('designs/bias_best.spice').read_text())
print('BEST',json.dumps(best),flush=True)

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path
rows=[];cases=[];revs=set()
for pvt in ['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']:
 for cap in [100,1000]:
  r=json.loads(Path(f'results/d15_{pvt}_{cap}.json').read_text())
  revs.add(r['revision'])
  for row in r['summary']['spec_table']:
   rows.append(dict(row,pvt=pvt,cap_nf=cap))
  cases.extend(r['cases'])
assert len(cases)==198 and len(revs)==1
assert all(r['verdict']=='PASS' for r in rows)

def worst(metric,case_filter=None,limit_filter=None):
 a=[r for r in rows if r['metric']==metric and (case_filter is None or r.get('case_id') in case_filter) and (limit_filter is None or r.get('limit')==limit_filter)]
 if not a:return None
 r=(min if a[0].get('direction')=='>=' else max)(a,key=lambda r:r['measured'])
 return {k:r.get(k) for k in ['metric','measured','limit','unit','case_id','pvt','cap_nf']}
summary={'revision':next(iter(revs)),'public_groups':18,'native_cases':198,'all_checks_passed':True,'worst':{}}
for name,metric,flt,lim in [
 ('dc_error','dc_error_v',None,None),
 ('light_overhead','overhead_a',None,1e-4),
 ('overhead','overhead_a',None,3e-4),
 ('reference_current','vref_abs_a',None,None),
 ('load_excursion','envelope_error_v',['light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'],None),
 ('line_excursion','envelope_error_v',['line'],None),
 ('psrr_1khz','psrr_1000_db',None,None),
 ('psrr_1mhz','psrr_1000000_db',None,None),
 ('noise','output_noise_rms_v',None,None)]:
 summary['worst'][name]=worst(metric,flt,lim)
recovery=[r for r in rows if r['metric'].startswith('recovery_') and r.get('measured') is not None]
w=max(recovery,key=lambda r:r['measured'])
summary['worst']['recovery']={k:w.get(k) for k in ['metric','measured','limit','unit','case_id','pvt','cap_nf']}
summary['resources']=json.loads(Path('results/final_validate.json').read_text())['normalized_params']['resources']
Path('results/final_summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))

 'light_overhead':('Light-load overhead',1e6,'uA'),
 'overhead':('Overhead at all loads',1e6,'uA'),
 'reference_current':('Reference current magnitude',1e6,'uA'),
 'load_excursion':('Load excursion',1e3,'mV'),
 'line_excursion':('Line excursion',1e3,'mV'),
 'psrr_1khz':('PSRR at 1 kHz',1,'dB'),
 'psrr_1mhz':('PSRR at 1 MHz',1,'dB'),
 'noise':('Output noise, 10 Hz to 100 kHz',1e6,'uV RMS'),
 'recovery':('Recovery',1e6,'us')}
for k,(label,scale,unit) in formats.items():
 w=s['worst'][k]
 lines.append(f\"| {label} | {w['measured']*scale:.4g} {unit} | {w['limit']*scale:.4g} {unit} |\")
lines.extend(['', 'Resources: 100 pF explicit capacitance; 6436.3 square micrometres MOS W*L proxy.', '', 'The fixed circuit uses a PMOS pass device, a low-current NMOS error amplifier with matched cascode drain control, a complementary gate buffer with load-dependent bias, and compensation referenced to VIN. A VIN/VREF divider biases the cascodes and draws at most 0.6 uA from VREF. The 0.18 um input devices and 0.3 um cascodes balance headroom against supply coupling. The standard measurement grid passed; independent evaluation occurs on submission.'])
Path('results/design_summary.md').write_text('\\n'.join(lines)+'\\n')
rationale=('Continuous-time PMOS LDO using a low-current NMOS error amplifier, 0.18 um input devices and 0.3 um cascodes to control input drain voltages while preserving operation at VIN=1.4 V. A complementary gate buffer uses a replica pass device to increase its bias with load. The VIN/VREF cascode divider draws at most 0.6 uA from VREF. Feedback uses 29.8 kohm/30 kohm resistors; compensation uses an 80 kohm series resistor with 60 pF to VIN and 40 pF of pass-gate supply feedforward. This unchanged revision passed all 198 public native cases across nine PVT conditions and both load-capacitor endpoints. Worst public results: DC error 3.267 mV, light-load overhead 83.379 uA, all-load overhead 184.546 uA, load excursion 34.968 mV, line excursion 13.123 mV, recovery 19.3203 us, output noise 22.054 uV RMS, minimum PSRR 40.555 dB at 1 kHz and 32.584 dB at 1 MHz. Validation reports 100 pF explicit capacitance and 6436.3 um2 MOS W*L proxy. Submit this fixed circuit for independent evaluation.')
Path('submit_request.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'submit','rationale':rationale},indent=2))
